1250                        assign   CS_INT   = ( During_RST_REC == 1'b0 && RESETB_INT == 1'b1 ) ? CS : 1'b1;
1251                        assign   WP_B_INT = (Status_Reg[6] == 1'b0 && ENQUAD == 1'b0) ? WP : 1'b1;
1252                        assign   HOLD_B_INT = (RPARAM[7] == 1'b0 && Status_Reg[6] == 1'b0 && ENQUAD == 1'b0 && CS_INT == 1'b0 && DTR_Mode == 1'b0) ? (SIO3===1'bz ? 1'b1 : SIO3) : 1'b1;
1253                        assign   RESETB_INT = (RPARAM[7] == 1'b1 && Status_Reg[6] == 1'b0 && ENQUAD == 1'b0) ? (SIO3===1'bz ? 1'b1 : SIO3) : 1'b1;

4337                        /*	Description: define a wait dummy cycle task			    */
4338                        /*	INPUT							            */
4339                        /*	    Cnum: cycle number						    */
4340                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
4341                        task dummy_cycle;
4342                    			input [31:0] Cnum;
4343                    			begin
4344       1/1          	    	repeat( Cnum ) begin
4345       2/2          					@ ( posedge SCLK_INT );
4346                    	    	end
4347                    			end
4348                        endtask // dummy_cycle

4372                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
4373                        /*	Description: define a read id task (RDJDID)			    */
4374                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
4375                        task read_jedecid;
4376                    			reg  [23:0] Dummy_ID;
4377                    			integer Dummy_Count;
4378                    			begin
4379       0/1     ==>  				Dummy_ID = {MID_ISSI, Memory_Type, Memory_Density};

4380       0/2     ==>  				if (ENQUAD) Dummy_Count = 6;

4381       0/1     ==>  				else Dummy_Count = 24;

4382       0/1     ==>  				forever begin

4383       0/2     ==>  					@ ( negedge SCLK_INT or posedge CS_INT );

4384       0/2     ==>  					if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) begin disable read_jedecid; end

4385                    					else begin // negedge SCLK_INT 
4386       0/1     ==>  						if (ENQUAD) begin

4387       0/1     ==>  							SI_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

4388       0/1     ==>  							WP_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

4389       0/1     ==>  							SIO3_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

4390                    						end // ENQUAD
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
4391       0/1     ==>  						SO_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

4392       0/1     ==>  						SO_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

4393       0/1     ==>  						SI_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

4394       0/1     ==>  						WP_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

4395       0/1     ==>  						SIO3_IN_EN= 1'b0;

4396       0/1     ==>  						if ( Dummy_Count ) begin

4397       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

4398       0/1     ==>  							if (ENQUAD) begin

4399       0/1     ==>  								if ( Dummy_Count == 5 )

4400       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[23:20];

4401       0/1     ==>  								else if ( Dummy_Count == 4 )

4402       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[19:16];

4403       0/1     ==>  								else if ( Dummy_Count == 3 )

4404       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[15:12];

4405       0/1     ==>  								else if ( Dummy_Count == 2 )

4406       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[11:8];

4407       0/1     ==>  								else if ( Dummy_Count == 1 )

4408       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[7:4];

4409       0/1     ==>  								else if ( Dummy_Count == 0 )

4410       0/1     ==>  									{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[3:0];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
4411                    							end // ENQUAD
4412                    							else begin
4413       0/1     ==>  								SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

4414                    							end // else
4415                    						end // Dummy_Count
4416                    						else begin
4417       0/1     ==>  							if (ENQUAD) begin

4418       0/1     ==>  								Dummy_Count = 5;

4419       0/1     ==>  								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[23:20];

4420                    							end // ENQUAD
4421                    							else begin
4422       0/1     ==>  								Dummy_Count = 23;

4423       0/1     ==>  								SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

4424                    							end // else
4425                    						end // else
4426                    					end // else // negedge SCLK_INT
4427                    				end  // end forever
4428                    			end
4429                        endtask // read_jedecid

4851                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
4852                        task read_1xio;
4853                    	integer Dummy_Count, Tmp_Int;
4854                    	reg  [7:0]	 OUT_Buf;
4855                    	  begin

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE

4865                    			  begin 
4866       1/1          				disable read_1xio; 
4867                    			  end

4877                    		    	  end

4883       0/1     ==>                			Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

4884       1/1                        		else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

5441                    	/*  Description: Execute program PPB register							*/
5442                    	/*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
5443                    	task program_ppb_register;
5444                          begin
5445                        	if ( PPBLB == 1'b0 ) 
5446                    		  begin
5447                        		//WIP : write in process Bit
5448                        		Status_Reg[0] = 1'b0;
5449                        		//WEL:Write Enable Latch
5450                        		Status_Reg[1] = 1'b0;
5451                        		exRPARAM[2] = 1'b1;
5452                        		WRPPB_Mode = 1'b0;
5453                        	  end
5454                        	else 
5455                    		  begin
5456                                Block  =  Address [A_MSB:16];
5457                                Status_Reg[0] = 1'b1;
5458                                #tBP;
5459                            	if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == 0 && TBPARAM) 
5460                    			  begin
5461                                    PPB_Reg_BOT0[Address[15:12]] = 1'b0;
5462                            	  end
5463                            	else if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == 1 && TBPARAM) 
5464                    			  begin
5465                                    PPB_Reg_BOT1[Address[15:12]] = 1'b0;
5466                            	  end
5467                            	else if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == Block_NUM-1 && !TBPARAM) 
5468                    			  begin

5478                                Status_Reg[0] = 1'b0;
5479                            	//WEL:Write Enable Latch
5480                                Status_Reg[1] = 1'b0;

5887                                	  end
5888                            	  end
5889                        	  end // end forever

5899                    	/*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
5900                    	task read_ppb_register;
5901                    	reg [7:0] PPB_Out;
5902                    	integer Dummy_Count;
5903                    	  begin
5904       0/1     ==>      	Dummy_Count = ENQUAD ? 2 : 8;

5905       0/1     ==>  		if ( (!BAR[7] && !ADD_4B_Mode) && !ENQUAD )

5906       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(24);						// SPI 3-byte address in

5907       0/1     ==>  		else if ( (BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode) && !ENQUAD )

5908       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(32);						// SPI 4-byte address in

5909       0/1     ==>  		else if ( (!BAR[7] && !ADD_4B_Mode) && ENQUAD )

5910       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(6);							// QPI 3-byte address in

5911       0/1     ==>  		else if ( (BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode) && ENQUAD )

5912       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(8);							// QPI 4-byte address in

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
5913       0/2     ==>      	#1;

5915       0/1     ==>      	if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == 0 && TBPARAM) 

5916                    		  begin
5917       0/1     ==>          	PPB_Out =  {8{PPB_Reg_BOT0[Address[15:12]]}};

5918                        	  end

5920                    		  begin
5921       0/1     ==>          	PPB_Out =  {8{PPB_Reg_BOT1[Address[15:12]]}};

5922                        	  end
5923       0/1     ==>      	else if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == Block_NUM-1 && !TBPARAM) 

5924                    		  begin
5925       0/1     ==>          	PPB_Out =  {8{PPB_Reg_TOP511[Address[15:12]]}};

5926                        	  end
5927       0/1     ==>      	else if (Block[Block_MSB:0] == Block_NUM-2 && !TBPARAM) 

5928                    		  begin
5929       0/1     ==>          	PPB_Out =  {8{PPB_Reg_TOP510[Address[15:12]]}};

5930                        	  end
5931                        	else 
5932                    		  begin
5933       0/1     ==>          	PPB_Out =  {8{PPB_Reg[Block]}};

5934                        	  end
5935       0/1     ==>      	forever 

5936                    		  begin
5937       0/2     ==>          	@ ( negedge SCLK or posedge CS_INT );

5938       0/1     ==>          	if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) 

5939                    			  begin
5940       0/1     ==>              	disable read_ppb_register;

5941                            	  end
5942                            	else 
5943                    			  begin
5944       0/1     ==>  				if (ENQUAD)

5945                    				  begin
5946       0/1     ==>  					SI_OUT_EN	= 1'b1;

5947       0/1     ==>  					WP_OUT_EN	= 1'b1;

5948       0/1     ==>  					SIO3_OUT_EN	= 1'b1;

5949                    				  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
5950       0/1     ==>              	SO_OUT_EN 	= 1'b1;

5951       0/1     ==>              	SI_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

5952       0/1     ==>              	SO_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

5953       0/1     ==>              	WP_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

5954       0/1     ==>              	SIO3_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

5955       0/1     ==>              	if ( Dummy_Count ) 

5956                    				  begin
5957       0/1     ==>                  	Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

5958       0/1     ==>  					if (ENQUAD)

5959                    					  begin
5960       0/1     ==>  						{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_Count ? PPB_Out[7:4] : PPB_Out[3:0];

5961                    					  end
5962                    					else
5963                    					  begin 
5964       0/1     ==>                  		SIO1_Reg <= #tV PPB_Out[Dummy_Count];

5965                    					  end
5966                                	  end
5967                                	else 
5968                    				  begin
5969       0/1     ==>  					if (ENQUAD)

5970                    					  begin

5972       0/1     ==>  						{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV PPB_Out[7:4];

5973                    					  end
5974                    					else
5975                    					  begin
5976       0/1     ==>                  		Dummy_Count = 7;

6093                    //			!(Block[Block_MSB:0] == 1 && TBPARAM && ((Address[15] && !SEC_Pro_Reg_BOT1[15:8]) || (!Address[15] && !SEC_Pro_Reg_BOT1[7:0]))) &&

6095                    //			!(Block[Block_MSB:0] == 510 && !TBPARAM && ((Address[15] && !SEC_Pro_Reg_TOP510[15:8]) || (!Address[15] && !SEC_Pro_Reg_TOP510[7:0]))) )

6099       0/1     ==>  			if ( sg_protect == 1'b0 )

6100                    		      begin
6101       0/1     ==>           		for( i = Start_Add; i <= End_Add; i = i + 1 )

6102                                   	  begin
6103       0/1     ==>                    	ARRAY[i] = 8'hxx;

6104                                   	  end

6123                                          end

6125                                      end

6128       0/1     ==>                 	Status_Reg[0] =  1'b0;//WIP

6138       0/1     ==>  				exRPARAM[1] = 1'b1; // PROT_E

6139                    			  end

6141                    		  end	// (write_protect(Address) == 1'b0)
6142                    		else

6144       0/2     ==>  			#10;

6146       0/1     ==>  			exRPARAM[1] = 1'b1; // PROT_E

6147       0/1     ==>  			Status_Reg[1] = 1'b0;	// WEL

6148                    		  end

6641                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6642                        /*	Description: define a page program task				    */
6643                        /*		     02 AD1 AD2 AD3	DAT ...				    */
6644                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6645                        task page_program;
6646                    			input  [A_MSB:0]  Address;
6647                    			reg    [7:0]	  Offset;
6648                    			integer Dummy_Count, Tmp_Int, i;
6649                    			begin
6650       0/1     ==>  	    	Dummy_Count = Buffer_Num;    // page size

6651       0/1     ==>  	    	Tmp_Int = 0;

6652       0/1     ==>          Offset  = Address[7:0];

6653                    	    /*------------------------------------------------*/
6654                    	    /*	Store 256 bytes into a temp buffer - Dummy_A  */
6655                    	    /*------------------------------------------------*/
6656       0/2     ==>        	for (i = 0; i < Dummy_Count ; i = i + 1 ) begin Dummy_A[i]  = 8'hff; end

6657                    
6658       0/1     ==>  	    	forever begin

6659       0/2     ==>  					@ ( posedge SCLK_INT or posedge CS_INT );

6660       0/1     ==>  					if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) begin

6661       0/1     ==>  		    		if ( (Tmp_Int % 8 !== 0) || (Tmp_Int == 1'b0) ) begin

6662       0/1     ==>  							PP_4XIO_Mode = 0;

6663       0/1     ==>  							PP_1XIO_Mode = 0;

6664       0/1     ==>  							disable page_program;		// exit for non-byte input (invalid)

6665                    		    		end
6666                    		    		else begin
6667       0/2     ==>  		        	if ( Tmp_Int > 8 ) Byte_PGM_Mode = 1'b0;

6668       0/1     ==>                else Byte_PGM_Mode = 1'b1;

6669       0/1     ==>  							update_array ( Address );

6670                    		    		end
6671       0/1     ==>  		    		disable page_program;			// exit for successfully finished program

6672                    					end		// CS_INT == 1'b1
6673                    					else begin  // count how many Bits been shifted
6674       0/1     ==>  		    		Tmp_Int = ( PP_4XIO_Mode | ENQUAD ) ? Tmp_Int + 4 : Tmp_Int + 1;

6675       0/1     ==>  		    		if ( Tmp_Int % 8 == 0) begin

6676       0/2     ==>              	#1;

6677       0/1     ==>  		        	Dummy_A[Offset] = SI_Reg [7:0];

6678       0/1     ==>  		        	Offset = Offset + 1;   

6679       0/1     ==>              	Offset = Offset[7:0];   

6680                                end  
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6681                    					end
6682                    	    	end  // end forever
6683                    			end
6684                        endtask // page_program

6687                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6688                        /*	Description: define a read electronic signature ID (RDESID)			    */
6689                        /*		     AB X X X						    */
6690                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6691                        task read_esid;
6692                    			reg  [7:0] Dummy_ID;
6693                    			integer Dummy_Count;
6694                    			begin
6695       0/1     ==>  				Dummy_Count = ENQUAD ? 2 : 8;

6696       0/2     ==>  				if (ENQUAD) begin dummy_cycle(5); end

6697       0/1     ==>  				else begin dummy_cycle(23); end

6698       0/1     ==>  				Dummy_ID = Device_ID;

6699       0/1     ==>  				dummy_cycle(1);

6700                    
6701       0/1     ==>  				forever begin

6702       0/2     ==>  					@ ( negedge SCLK_INT or posedge CS_INT );

6703       0/2     ==>  					if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) begin disable read_esid; end 

6704                    					else begin  
6705       0/1     ==>  						if (ENQUAD) begin

6706       0/1     ==>  							SI_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

6707       0/1     ==>  							WP_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

6708       0/1     ==>  							SIO3_OUT_EN  = 1'b1;

6709                    						end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6710       0/1     ==>  						SO_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

6711       0/1     ==>  						SO_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6712       0/1     ==>  						SI_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6713       0/1     ==>  						WP_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6714       0/1     ==>  						SIO3_IN_EN= 1'b0;

6715       0/1     ==>  						if ( Dummy_Count ) begin

6716       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

6717       0/1     ==>  							if (ENQUAD) begin

6718       0/1     ==>  								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_Count ? Dummy_ID[7:4] : Dummy_ID[3:0];

6719                    							end
6720                    							else begin
6721       0/1     ==>  								SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

6722                    							end
6723                    						end
6724                    						else begin
6725       0/1     ==>  							if (ENQUAD) begin

6726       0/1     ==>  								Dummy_Count = 1;

6727       0/1     ==>  								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[7:4];

6728                    							end
6729                    							else begin
6730       0/1     ==>  								Dummy_Count = 7;

6731       0/1     ==>  								SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

6732                    							end
6733                    		   			end
6734                    					end
6735                    				end // end forever	 
6736                    			end
6737                        endtask // read_esid

6740                        /*	Description: define a read electronic manufacturer & device ID	    */
6741                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6742                        task read_mdid;
6743                    	reg  [15:0] Dummy_ID;
6744                    	integer Dummy_Count;
6745                    	begin
6746       0/2     ==>  		if (ENQUAD) Dummy_Count = 4;

6747       0/1     ==>  		else Dummy_Count = 16;

6748       0/2     ==>  		if (ENQUAD) dummy_cycle(6);

6749       0/1     ==>  		else dummy_cycle(24);

6750       0/2     ==>  		#1;

6751       0/1     ==>  		if ( Address[0] == 1'b0 ) begin

6752       0/1     ==>  			Dummy_ID = {MID_ISSI,Device_ID};

6753                    		end
6754                    		else begin
6755       0/1     ==>  			Dummy_ID = {Device_ID,MID_ISSI};

6756                    		end
6757       0/1     ==>  		forever begin

6758       0/2     ==>  			@ ( negedge SCLK_INT or posedge CS_INT );

6759       0/1     ==>  			if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) begin

6760       0/1     ==>  				disable read_mdid;

6761                    			end
6762                    			else begin
6763       0/1     ==>  				if (ENQUAD) begin

6764       0/1     ==>  					SI_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

6765       0/1     ==>  					WP_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

6766       0/1     ==>  					SIO3_OUT_EN  = 1'b1;

6767                    				end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6768       0/1     ==>  				SO_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

6769       0/1     ==>  				SO_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6770       0/1     ==>  				SI_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6771       0/1     ==>  				WP_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

6772       0/1     ==>  				SIO3_IN_EN= 1'b0;

6773       0/1     ==>  				if ( Dummy_Count ) begin

6774       0/1     ==>  					Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

6775       0/1     ==>  					if (ENQUAD) begin

6776       0/1     ==>  						if ( Dummy_Count == 3 )

6777       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[15:12];

6778       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 2 )

6779       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[11:8];

6780       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 1 )

6781       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[7:4];

6782       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 0 )

6783       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[3:0];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6784                    					end
6785                    					else begin
6786       0/1     ==>  						SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

6787                    					end
6788                    				end
6789                    				else begin
6790       0/1     ==>  					if (ENQUAD) begin

6791       0/1     ==>  						Dummy_Count = 3;

6792       0/1     ==>  						{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_ID[15:12];

6793                    					end
6794                    					else begin
6795       0/1     ==>  						Dummy_Count = 15;

6796       0/1     ==>  						SIO1_Reg <= #tV Dummy_ID[Dummy_Count];

6797                    					end
6798                    				end
6799                    			end
6800                    		end	// end forever
6801                    	end
6802                        endtask // read_mdid
6803                    
6804                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6805                        /*	Description: define a program chip task				    */
6806                        /*	INPUT:address                            			    */
6807                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6808                        task update_array;
6809                    	input [A_MSB:0] Address;
6810                    	integer Dummy_Count, i, j;
6811                        integer program_time;
6812                        reg [7:0]  ori [0:Buffer_Num-1];
6813                    	reg [A_MSB:0] A_tmp;
6814                      	reg [A_MSB_SG:0] A_SG_tmp;
6815                    	//wire [7:0] ARRAY5 = ARRAY[5];
6816                    	  begin
6817       0/1     ==>  	   	Dummy_Count = Buffer_Num;

6818       0/1     ==>          A_tmp = { Address [A_MSB:8], 8'h00 };

6819                    //		A_SG_tmp = { Address [A_MSB_SG:8], 8'h00 };
6820       0/1     ==>    		A_SG_tmp = 8'h00 ;

6821       0/1     ==>          program_time = (Byte_PGM_Mode) ? tBP : tPP;

6822       0/1     ==>  		if ( write_protect(A_tmp) == 1'b0 ) 

6823                    		  begin
6824       0/1     ==>  	    	Status_Reg[0]= 1'b1;

6825       0/1     ==>    			safeguard(A_tmp, sg_protect);

6826       0/1     ==>    	   		if ( sg_protect == 1'b0 ) 

6827                    			  begin
6828       0/1     ==>          		for ( i = 0; i < Dummy_Count; i = i + 1 ) 

6829                    				  begin
6830       0/1     ==>            			if ( SIR_Mode == 1'b1) 

6831                    					  begin
6832       0/1     ==>  						if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b00) 

6833                    						  begin
6834       0/1     ==>              				ori[i] = SIR0_ARRAY[i];

6835       0/1     ==>              				SIR0_ARRAY[i] = SIR0_ARRAY[i] & 8'bx;

6836                    						  end
6837       0/1     ==>  						else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b01) 

6838                    						  begin
6839       0/1     ==>              				ori[i] = SIR1_ARRAY[i];

6840       0/1     ==>              				SIR1_ARRAY[i] = SIR1_ARRAY[i] & 8'bx;

6841                    						  end
6842       0/1     ==>  						else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b10) 

6843                    						  begin
6844       0/1     ==>              				ori[i] = SIR2_ARRAY[i];

6845       0/1     ==>              				SIR2_ARRAY[i] = SIR2_ARRAY[i] & 8'bx;

6846                    						  end
6847       0/1     ==>  						else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b11) 

6848                    						  begin
6849       0/1     ==>              				ori[i] = SIR3_ARRAY[i];

6850       0/1     ==>              				SIR3_ARRAY[i] = SIR3_ARRAY[i] & 8'bx;

6851                    						  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6852                                		  end // SIR_Mode
6853       0/1     ==>             			else if ( SG_Mode == 1'b1 ) 

6854                      					  begin
6855       0/1     ==>             				ori[i] = SG_ARRAY[A_SG_tmp + i];

6856       0/1     ==>             				SG_ARRAY[A_SG_tmp+ i] = SG_ARRAY[A_SG_tmp + i] & 8'bx;

6857                               			  end // SG_Mode
6858                                		else 
6859                    					  begin
6860       0/1     ==>              			ori[i] = ARRAY[A_tmp + i];

6861       0/1     ==>              			ARRAY[A_tmp+ i] = ARRAY[A_tmp + i] & 8'bx;

6862                                		  end
6863                              		  end // for loop
6864       0/1     ==>      	      	fork pg_timer;

6865                    				  begin
6866       0/1     ==>      	      		for( j = 0; j*2 < program_time; j = j + 1 ) 

6867                    					  begin
6868       0/2     ==>      	       			@ ( negedge PGM_CLK or posedge Susp_Trig );

6869       0/1     ==>      	       			if ( Susp_Trig == 1'b1 ) 

6870                    						  begin
6871       0/1     ==>      	       				if (SG_Mode == 1'b1) 

6872       0/1     ==>  								suspend_sg_program;

6873       0/1     ==>      	       				else if (SIR_Mode == 1'b1) 

6874       0/1     ==>  								suspend_sir_program;

6875                    							else 
6876       0/1     ==>  								suspend_program;

6877       0/1     ==>      	       				$display ( $time, " Resume program ..." );

6878                        	        		  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6879                        	      		  end
6880                        	      		//#program_time ;
6881       0/1     ==>      	      		for ( i = 0; i < Dummy_Count; i = i + 1 ) 

6882                    					  begin
6883       0/1     ==>      	      			if ( SIR_Mode == 1'b1) 

6884                    						  begin
6885       0/2     ==>  							if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b00) SIR0_ARRAY[i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

6886       0/2     ==>  							else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b01) SIR1_ARRAY[i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

6887       0/2     ==>  							else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b10) SIR2_ARRAY[i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

6888       0/2     ==>  							else if (A_tmp[13:12] == 2'b11) SIR3_ARRAY[i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
6889                    						  end	// SIR_Mode
6890       0/1     ==>      	      			else if (SG_Mode == 1'b1) 

6891                    						  begin
6892       0/1     ==>      	      				SG_ARRAY[A_SG_tmp+ i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

6893                      						  end
6894                        	      			else 
6895                    						  begin
6896       0/1     ==>      	      				ARRAY[A_tmp+ i] = ori[i] & Dummy_A[i];

6897                    						  end
6898                        	      		  end
6899       0/1     ==>      	      		disable pg_timer;

6900                        	   	 	  end // begin of fork
6901                        	      	join // fork
6902                    		   	  end // (sg_protect == 1'b0)
6903                    		   	else 
6904                    			  begin		// (sg_protect == 1'b1)
6905       0/2     ==>    		  		#tPGM_CHK ;

6906       0/1     ==>  				exRPARAM[2] = 1'b1; // P_ERR

6907       0/1     ==>  				exRPARAM[1] = 1'b1; // PROT_E

6908                      		  	  end
6909       0/1     ==>  		   	Status_Reg[1] = 1'b0;

6910                    		  end		// (write_protect(A_tmp) == 1'b0
6911                    		else 
6912                    		  begin 	// (write_protect(A_tmp) == 1'b1
6913       0/2     ==>  			#10;

6914       0/1     ==>  			exRPARAM[2] = 1'b1; // P_ERR

6915       0/1     ==>  			exRPARAM[1] = 1'b1; // PROT_E

6916                    		  end
6917       0/1     ==>     		Status_Reg[0] = 1'b0;

6918       0/1     ==>     		Status_Reg[1] = 1'b0;

6919       0/1     ==>     		PP_4XIO_Mode = 1'b0;

6920       0/1     ==>     		PP_1XIO_Mode = 1'b0;

6921       0/1     ==>     		Byte_PGM_Mode = 1'b0;

6922       0/1     ==>  		SIR_Mode = 1'b0;

6923       0/1     ==>    		SG_Mode = 1'b0;

6924       0/1     ==>  		WREN_06CMD		=  1'b0;

6925                    	  end 
6926                        endtask // update_array
6927

6936       0/1     ==>              Status_Reg[0] =  1'b0;//WIP

6937       0/1     ==>              Status_Reg[1] =  1'b0;//WEL

6938       0/1     ==>  						WREN_06CMD		=  1'b0;

6939       0/1     ==>              FR_Reg[2]  =  1'b1;

6940       0/1     ==>              WR2Susp = 0;

6941       0/2     ==>              @ ( Resume_Event );

6942       0/1     ==>              Status_Reg[0] =  1'b1;//WIP

6943                                //Status_Reg[1] =  1'b1;//WEL
6944       0/1     ==>              FR_Reg[2]  =  1'b0;

6945                            end

6993                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6994                        /*  Description: define a timer to count program time                   */
6995                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
6996                        task pg_timer;
6997                            begin
6998       0/1     ==>              PGM_CLK = 1'b0;

6999       0/1     ==>              forever

7000                                    begin
7001       0/2     ==>                      #1 PGM_CLK = ~PGM_CLK;    // program timer period is 2ns

7002                                    end
7003                            end
7004                        endtask // pg_timer

7006                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7007                        /*  Description: Execute Read Bank Address Register						*/
7008                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7009                        task read_BA_Register;
7010                        integer Dummy_Count;
7011                          begin
7012       0/1     ==>  		if (ENQUAD) 

7013                    		  begin
7014       0/1     ==>              Dummy_Count = 2;

7015                              end
7016                            else 
7017                    		  begin
7018       0/1     ==>              Dummy_Count = 8;

7019                              end
7020       0/2     ==>          forever @ ( negedge SCLK or posedge CS_INT ) 

7021                    		  begin // output Reading Bank Address register info
7022       0/1     ==>          	if ( CS_INT == 1 ) 

7023                    			  begin
7024       0/1     ==>              	disable read_BA_Register;

7025                            	  end
7026                             	else  
7027                    			  begin
7028       0/1     ==>                  if (ENQUAD) 

7029                    				  begin
7030       0/1     ==>                      SI_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

7031       0/1     ==>                      WP_OUT_EN    = 1'b1;

7032       0/1     ==>                      SIO3_OUT_EN  = 1'b1;

7033                                      end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7034       0/1     ==>              	SO_OUT_EN 	= 1'b1;

7035       0/1     ==>                  SO_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

7036       0/1     ==>                  SI_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

7037       0/1     ==>                  WP_IN_EN  	= 1'b0;

7038       0/1     ==>                  SIO3_IN_EN	= 1'b0;

7039       0/1     ==>              	if ( Dummy_Count ) 

7040                    				  begin
7041       0/1     ==>              		Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

7042       0/1     ==>                      if (ENQUAD) 

7043                    					  begin
7044       0/1     ==>                          {SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_Count ? BAR[7:4] : BAR[3:0];

7045                                          end
7046                                       	else 
7047                    					  begin
7048       0/1     ==>                          SIO1_Reg    <= #tV BAR[Dummy_Count];

7049                                          end
7050                                	  end
7051                                	else 
7052                    				  begin
7053       0/1     ==>                      if (ENQUAD) 

7054                    					  begin
7055       0/1     ==>                          Dummy_Count = 1;

7056       0/1     ==>                          {SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV BAR[7:4];

7057                                          end
7058                                        else 
7059                    					  begin
7060       0/1     ==>                          Dummy_Count = 7;

7061       0/1     ==>                          SIO1_Reg    <= #tV BAR[Dummy_Count];

7062                                          end
7063                                	  end
7064                            	  end
7065                    		  end // forever
7066                          end
7067                        endtask // read_BA_Register

7069                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7070                        /*  Description: Execute Write Bank Address Register					*/
7071                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7072                    	task write_ba_register;
7073                        reg [7:0] BA_Reg_Up;
7074                          begin
7075       0/1     ==>          WRBAR_Mode       = 1'b1;

7076                            //$display( $time, " Old Bank Address Register = %b", BAR );
7077       0/1     ==>          BA_Reg_Up = SI_Reg[7:0];

7078                    
7079       0/1     ==>          Status_Reg[0]   = 1'b1;

7080       0/2     ==>          #tW;

7081       0/1     ==>          BAR_NV[0]   =  BA_Reg_Up[0];

7082       0/1     ==>          BAR_NV[6:1] =  6'b000000;

7083       0/1     ==>          BAR_NV[7]   =  BA_Reg_Up[7];

7084       0/1     ==>          BAR[0]   =  BAR_NV[0];

7085       0/1     ==>          BAR[6:1] =  6'b000000;

7086       0/1     ==>          BAR[7]   =  BAR_NV[7];

7087                    
7088                            //WIP:Write Enable Latch
7089       0/1     ==>          Status_Reg[0]   = 1'b0;

7090                            //WEL:Write Enable Latch
7091       0/1     ==>          Status_Reg[1]   = 1'b0;

7092       0/1     ==>          WRBAR_Mode       = 1'b0;

7093                          end
7094                    	endtask // write_ba_register

7097                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7098                        /*	Description: Execute 2X IO Read Mode				    */
7099                        /*		     BB AD1 AD2 AD3	DMY DAT ...				    */
7100                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7101                        task read_2xio;
7102                    	reg  [7:0]  OUT_Buf;
7103                    	integer     Dummy_Count;
7104                    	  begin
7105       0/1     ==>  	   	Dummy_Count=4;

7106       0/1     ==>  	   	SI_IN_EN = 1'b1;

7107       0/1     ==>  	   	SO_IN_EN = 1'b1;

7108       0/1     ==>  	   	SI_OUT_EN = 1'b0;

7109       0/1     ==>  	   	SO_OUT_EN = 1'b0;

7110       0/1     ==>  	   	if ( !BAR[7] && !ADD_4B_Mode ) 

7111       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(12); // for 3-byte address in

7112       0/1     ==>  	   	else if ( BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode ) 

7113       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(16); // for 4-byte address in

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7114                    
7115                    		  begin
7116                    			fork
7117                    			  begin
7118       0/1     ==>  				if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0000 )

7119                    				  begin
7120       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7121                    				  end
7122       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0001 )

7123                    				  begin
7124       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(1);

7125                    				  end
7126       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0010 )

7127                    				  begin
7128       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(2);

7129                    				  end
7130       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0011 )

7131                    				  begin
7132       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(3);

7133                    				  end
7134       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0100 )

7135                    				  begin
7136       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7137                    				  end
7138       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0101 )

7139                    				  begin
7140       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(5);

7141                    				  end
7142       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0110 )

7143                    				  begin
7144       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(6);

7145                    				  end
7146       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0111 )

7147                    				  begin
7148       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(7);

7149                    				  end
7150       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1000 )

7151                    				  begin
7152       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(8);

7153                    				  end
7154       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1001 )

7155                    				  begin
7156       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(9);

7157                    				  end
7158       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1010 )

7159                    				  begin
7160       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(10);

7161                    				  end
7162       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1011 )

7163                    				  begin
7164       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(11);

7165                    				  end
7166       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1100 )

7167                    				  begin
7168       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(12);

7169                    				  end
7170       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1101 )

7171                    				  begin
7172       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(13);

7173                    				  end
7174       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1110 )

7175                    				  begin
7176       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(14);

7177                    				  end
7178       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1111 )

7179                    				  begin
7180       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(15);

7181                    				  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7182                    //	    	dummy_cycle(4);		// first DMY byte: AX input for BBh command
7183       0/2     ==>  	    		#1;

7184       0/1     ==>  	    		if (RDDIO_Mode && (SI_Reg[7:4] == 4'hA)) 

7185       0/1     ==>  					Set_2XIO_Enhance_Mode = 1'b1;	// RDDIO_Mode is BBh read only

7186                    	    		else	
7187       0/1     ==>  					Set_2XIO_Enhance_Mode = 1'b0; 

7188                    				// Dummy Clock
7189                    //        if ( RPARAM[4] == 1'b1 ) dummy_cycle(4);
7190       0/2     ==>  				#1;

7191                    //				Prea_OUT_EN2 <= #tV 1'b0;
7192       0/1     ==>  				read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7193                              
7194       0/2     ==>  	    		forever @ ( negedge SCLK_INT or  posedge CS_INT ) 

7195                    				  begin
7196       0/1     ==>  	      			if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) 

7197                    					  begin 
7198       0/1     ==>  						disable read_2xio; 

7199                    					  end
7200                    	        		else 
7201                    					  begin
7202       0/1     ==>  		    			Read_Mode	= 1'b1;

7203       0/1     ==>  		    			SO_OUT_EN	= 1'b1;

7204       0/1     ==>  		    			SI_OUT_EN	= 1'b1;

7205       0/1     ==>  		    			SI_IN_EN	= 1'b0;

7206       0/1     ==>  		    			SO_IN_EN	= 1'b0;

7207       0/1     ==>  		    			if ( Dummy_Count ) 

7208                    						  begin
7209       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

7210       0/1     ==>  							if ( Dummy_Count == 3 )

7211                    //								if (DTR_Mode)
7212                    //									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[7:6] ;
7213                    //								else
7214       0/1     ==>  									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #tV OUT_Buf[7:6];

7215       0/1     ==>  							else if ( Dummy_Count == 2 )

7216                    //								if (DTR_Mode)
7217                    //									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[5:4] ;
7218                    //								else
7219       0/1     ==>  									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #tV OUT_Buf[5:4];

7220       0/1     ==>  							else if ( Dummy_Count == 1 )

7221                    //								if (DTR_Mode)
7222                    //									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[3:2] ;
7223                    //								else
7224       0/1     ==>  									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #tV OUT_Buf[3:2];

7225       0/1     ==>  							else if ( Dummy_Count == 0 )

7226                    //								if (DTR_Mode)
7227                    //									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[1:0] ;
7228                    //								else
7229       0/1     ==>  									{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #tV OUT_Buf[1:0];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7230                    		    			  end
7231                    		    			else 
7232                    						  begin
7233       0/1     ==>              				if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b00) && (Address[2:0]==3'b111) )

7234       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:3], 3'b000};

7235       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b01) && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7236       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7237       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7238       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7239       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b10) && (Address[4:0]==5'b1_1111) )

7240       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:5], 5'b0_0000};

7241       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b11) && (Address[5:0]==6'b11_1111) )

7242       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:6], 6'b00_0000};

7243                                  				else
7244       0/1     ==>                					Address = Address + 1;

7245                    
7246       0/1     ==>                				load_address(Address);

7247       0/1     ==>                				read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7248       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = 3;

7249                    //							if (DTR_Mode)
7250                    //							 	{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[7:6] ;
7251                    //							else
7252       0/1     ==>  							 	{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} = #tV OUT_Buf[7:6];

7253                    		    			  end
7254                    	        		  end
7255                    	    			end//forever  
7256                    
7257                    //			  begin
7258                    //				if (CR[4] )
7259                    //				  begin
7260                    //					dummy_cycle_prea(2);
7261                    //					Prea_OUT_EN2 = 1'b1;
7262                    //					preamble_bit_out;
7263                    //				  end
7264                    //			  end
7265                    
7266                    			  end
7267                    			join
7268                    		  end
7269                    	  end
7270                        endtask // read_2xio

7272                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7273                        /*	Description: Execute 4X IO Read Mode				    */
7274                    		/*	spi EB AD1 AD2 AD3 DMY... DAT...								*/
7275                    		/*	qpi XX AD1 AD2 AD3 DMY... DAT...								*/
7276                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7277                        task read_4xio;
7278                    	//reg [A_MSB:0] Address;
7279                    	reg [7:0]   OUT_Buf ;
7280                    	integer	    Dummy_Count;
7281                    	  begin
7282       0/1     ==>  	   	Dummy_Count = 2;

7283       0/1     ==>  	   	SI_OUT_EN   = 1'b0;

7284       0/1     ==>  	   	SO_OUT_EN   = 1'b0;

7285       0/1     ==>  	   	WP_OUT_EN   = 1'b0;

7286       0/1     ==>  	   	SIO3_OUT_EN = 1'b0;

7287       0/1     ==>  	   	SI_IN_EN	= 1'b1;

7288       0/1     ==>  	   	SO_IN_EN	= 1'b1;

7289       0/1     ==>  	   	WP_IN_EN	= 1'b1;

7290       0/1     ==>  	   	SIO3_IN_EN  = 1'b1;

7291                    //  	if ( BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode || CMD_BUS == READ4X4B || (!RDQIO_Mode && (CMD_BUS == RSTEN || CMD_BUS == RST) && EN4XIO_Read_Mode == 1'b1) ) 
7292                    //		  begin
7293                    //	   		ADD_4B_Mode = 1'b1;
7294                    //			dummy_cycle(8);		// for 4-byte address in
7295                    //		  end
7296                    //	   	else 
7297       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(6);		// for 3-byte address in

7298                    
7299       0/1     ==>  		if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0001 )

7300                    		  begin
7301       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(1);

7302       0/1     ==>  			Set_4XIO_Enhance_Mode = 1'b0; 

7303                    		  end
7304                    		else
7305       0/1     ==>  	   		dummy_cycle(2);		// first DMY byte: AX input for EBh command

7306       0/2     ==>  	   	#1;

7307       0/1     ==>  	   	if (RDQIO_Mode && (SI_Reg[7:4] == 4'hA)) 

7308       0/1     ==>  			Set_4XIO_Enhance_Mode = 1'b1;	// RDQIO_Mode is EBh read only

7309                    	   	else	
7310       0/1     ==>  			Set_4XIO_Enhance_Mode = 1'b0; 

7311                    
7312                    		  begin
7313                    			fork
7314                    			  begin	
7315                    				// Dummy Clock
7316       0/1     ==>          		if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0000 )

7317                    				  begin
7318       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7319                    				  end
7320       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0001 )

7321                    				  begin
7322       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(0);

7323                    				  end
7324       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0010 )

7325                    				  begin
7326       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(0);

7327                    				  end
7328       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0011 )

7329                    				  begin
7330       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(1);

7331                    				  end
7332       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0100 )

7333                    				  begin
7334       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(2);

7335                    				  end
7336       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0101 )

7337                    				  begin
7338       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(3);

7339                    				  end
7340       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0110 )

7341                    				  begin
7342       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7343                    				  end
7344       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0111 )

7345                    				  begin
7346       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(5);

7347                    				  end
7348       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1000 )

7349                    				  begin
7350       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(6);

7351                    				  end
7352       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1001 )

7353                    				  begin
7354       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(7);

7355                    				  end
7356       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1010 )

7357                    				  begin
7358       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(8);

7359                    				  end
7360       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1011 )

7361                    				  begin
7362       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(9);

7363                    				  end
7364       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1100 )

7365                    				  begin
7366       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(10);

7367                    				  end
7368       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1101 )

7369                    				  begin
7370       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(11);

7371                    				  end
7372       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1110 )

7373                    				  begin
7374       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(12);

7375                    				  end
7376       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1111 )

7377                    				  begin
7378       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(13);

7379                    				  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7380                    
7381                    //				Prea_OUT_EN4 <= #tV 1'b0;
7382       0/1     ==>          		read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7383                    
7384       0/2     ==>  	    		forever @ ( negedge SCLK_INT or  posedge CS_INT ) 

7385                    				  begin
7386       0/1     ==>  	        		if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) 

7387                    					  begin 
7388       0/1     ==>  						disable read_4xio; 

7389       0/1     ==>  						SIR_Mode = 1'b0;

7390       0/1     ==>    						SG_Mode = 1'b0;

7391                    					  end
7392                    	        		else 
7393                    					  begin
7394       0/1     ==>            				SO_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7395       0/1     ==>            				SI_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7396       0/1     ==>            				WP_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7397       0/1     ==>            				SIO3_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

7398       0/1     ==>            				SO_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7399       0/1     ==>            				SI_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7400       0/1     ==>            				WP_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7401       0/1     ==>            				SIO3_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

7402       0/1     ==>            				Read_Mode  	= 1'b1;

7403       0/1     ==>              			if ( Dummy_Count ) 

7404                    						  begin
7405       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

7406                    //							if ( DTR_Mode ) 
7407                    //								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #(tV-3) Dummy_Count ? OUT_Buf[7:4] : OUT_Buf[3:0];
7408                    //							else
7409       0/1     ==>  								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_Count ? OUT_Buf[7:4] : OUT_Buf[3:0];

7410                                			  end
7411                                			else 
7412                    						  begin
7413       0/1     ==>              				if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b00) && (Address[2:0]==3'b111) )

7414       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:3], 3'b000};

7415       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b01) && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7416       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7417       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7418       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7419       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b10) && (Address[4:0]==5'b1_1111) )

7420       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:5], 5'b0_0000};

7421       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b11) && (Address[5:0]==6'b11_1111) )

7422       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:6], 6'b00_0000};

7423                                  				else
7424       0/1     ==>                					Address = Address + 1;

7425       0/1     ==>               				load_address(Address);

7426       0/1     ==>               				read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7427       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = 1;

7428                    //							if (DTR_Mode)
7429                    //								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #(tV-3) OUT_Buf[7:4];
7430                    //							else
7431       0/1     ==>  								{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[7:4];

7432                    						  end
7433                    					  end
7434                    				  end//forever  
7435                    			  end
7436                    
7437                    //            begin
7438                    //                if ( CR[4] ) begin
7439                    //                    Prea_OUT_EN4 = 1'b1;
7440                    //                    preamble_bit_out;
7441                    //                end
7442                    //            end
7443                    			join
7444                    		  end
7445                    	  end
7446                        endtask // read_4xio

7448                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7449                        /*	Description: define a fast read dual output data task		    */
7450                        /*		     3B AD1 AD2 AD3 DMY DAT ...					    */
7451                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7452                        task fastread_2xio;
7453                    	integer Dummy_Count;
7454                    	reg  [7:0] OUT_Buf;
7455                    	  begin
7456       0/1     ==>  	   	Dummy_Count = 4 ;

7457       0/1     ==>  		if ( !BAR[7] && !ADD_4B_Mode )

7458       0/1     ==>  	     	dummy_cycle(24); 		// for 3-byte address in

7459       0/1     ==>  		else if ( BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode )

7460       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(32);		// for 4-byte address in

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7461                    
7462                    		  begin
7463                    		  fork
7464                    		    begin
7465                    			  begin
7466       0/1     ==>  				if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0000 )

7467                    				  begin
7468       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(8);

7469                    				  end
7470       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0001 )

7471                    				  begin
7472       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(1);

7473                    				  end
7474       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0010 )

7475                    				  begin
7476       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(2);

7477                    				  end
7478       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0011 )

7479                    				  begin
7480       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(3);

7481                    				  end
7482       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0100 )

7483                    				  begin
7484       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7485                    				  end
7486       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0101 )

7487                    				  begin
7488       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(5);

7489                    				  end
7490       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0110 )

7491                    				  begin
7492       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(6);

7493                    				  end
7494       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0111 )

7495                    				  begin
7496       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(7);

7497                    				  end
7498       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1000 )

7499                    				  begin
7500       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(8);

7501                    				  end
7502       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1001 )

7503                    				  begin
7504       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(9);

7505                    				  end
7506       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1010 )

7507                    				  begin
7508       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(10);

7509                    				  end
7510       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1011 )

7511                    				  begin
7512       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(11);

7513                    				  end
7514       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1100 )

7515                    				  begin
7516       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(12);

7517                    				  end
7518       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1101 )

7519                    				  begin
7520       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(13);

7521                    				  end
7522       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1110 )

7523                    				  begin
7524       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(14);

7525                    				  end
7526       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1111 )

7527                    				  begin
7528       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(15);

7529                    				  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7530                    			  end	
7531                    //			Prea_OUT_EN2 <= #tV 1'b0;
7532       0/1     ==>         		read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7533       0/2     ==>  	    	forever @ ( negedge SCLK or  posedge CS_INT ) 

7534                    			  begin
7535       0/1     ==>  	      		if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) 

7536                    				  begin 
7537       0/1     ==>  					disable fastread_2xio; 

7538                    				  end
7539                    	        	else 
7540                    				  begin
7541       0/1     ==>  		    		Read_Mode = 1'b1;

7542       0/1     ==>  		    		SO_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

7543       0/1     ==>  		    		SI_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

7544       0/1     ==>  		    		SI_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

7545       0/1     ==>  		    		SO_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

7546       0/1     ==>  		    		if ( Dummy_Count ) 

7547                    					  begin
7548       0/1     ==>  						Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

7549       0/1     ==>  						if ( Dummy_Count == 3 )

7550       0/1     ==>  							{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[7:6];

7551       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 2 )

7552       0/1     ==>  							{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[5:4];

7553       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 1 )

7554       0/1     ==>  							{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[3:2];

7555       0/1     ==>  						else if ( Dummy_Count == 0 )

7556       0/1     ==>  							{SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[1:0];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7557                    		    		  end
7558                    		    		else 
7559                    					  begin
7560       0/1     ==>              			if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b00) && (Address[2:0]==3'b111) )

7561       0/1     ==>                				Address = {Address[A_MSB:3], 3'b000};

7562       0/1     ==>                			else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b01) && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7563       0/1     ==>                				Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7564       0/1     ==>                			else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7565       0/1     ==>                				Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7566       0/1     ==>                			else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b10) && (Address[4:0]==5'b1_1111) )

7567       0/1     ==>                				Address = {Address[A_MSB:5], 5'b0_0000};

7568       0/1     ==>                			else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b11) && (Address[5:0]==6'b11_1111) )

7569       0/1     ==>                				Address = {Address[A_MSB:6], 6'b00_0000};

7570                                  			else
7571       0/1     ==>                				Address = Address + 1;

7572       0/1     ==>              			load_address(Address);

7573       0/1     ==>                			read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7574       0/1     ==>  						Dummy_Count = 3;

7581                    //		  begin

7583                    //			  begin

7585                    //				Prea_OUT_EN2 = 1'b1;

7587                    //			  end

7594                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7595                        /*	Description: define a fast read quad output data task		    */
7596                        /*		     6B AD1 AD2 AD3 DMY DAT ...					    */
7597                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
7598                        task fastread_4xio;
7599                    	integer Dummy_Count;
7600                    	reg  [7:0] OUT_Buf;
7601                    	  begin
7602       0/1     ==>  	   	Dummy_Count = 2 ;

7603       0/1     ==>  		if ( !BAR[7] && !ADD_4B_Mode )

7604       0/1     ==>  		    dummy_cycle(24); 		// for 3-byte address in

7605       0/1     ==>  		else if ( BAR[7] || ADD_4B_Mode )

7606       0/1     ==>  			dummy_cycle(32);		// for 4-byte address in

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE

7610                    			  begin
7611       0/1     ==>  				if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0000 )

7614                    				  end
7615       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0001 )

7616                    				  begin
7617       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(1);

7618                    				  end
7619       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0010 )

7620                    				  begin
7621       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(2);

7622                    				  end
7623       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0011 )

7624                    				  begin
7625       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(3);

7626                    				  end
7627       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0100 )

7628                    				  begin
7629       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(4);

7630                    				  end
7631       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0101 )

7632                    				  begin
7633       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(5);

7634                    				  end
7635       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0110 )

7636                    				  begin
7637       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(6);

7638                    				  end
7639       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b0111 )

7640                    				  begin
7641       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(7);

7642                    				  end
7643       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1000 )

7644                    				  begin
7645       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(8);

7646                    				  end
7647       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1001 )

7648                    				  begin
7649       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(9);

7650                    				  end
7651       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1010 )

7652                    				  begin
7653       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(10);

7654                    				  end
7655       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1011 )

7656                    				  begin
7657       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(11);

7658                    				  end
7659       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1100 )

7660                    				  begin
7661       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(12);

7662                    				  end
7663       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1101 )

7664                    				  begin
7665       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(13);

7666                    				  end
7667       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1110 )

7668                    				  begin
7669       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(14);

7670                    				  end
7671       0/1     ==>  				else if ( RPARAM[6:3] == 4'b1111 )

7672                    				  begin
7673       0/1     ==>  					dummy_cycle(15);

7674                    				  end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
7675                    
7676                    //				Prea_OUT_EN4 <= #tV 1'b0;
7677       0/1     ==>          		read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7678                    
7679       0/2     ==>  	    		forever @ ( negedge SCLK_INT or  posedge CS_INT ) 

7680                    				  begin
7681       0/1     ==>  	        		if ( CS_INT == 1'b1 ) 

7682                    					  begin 
7683       0/1     ==>  						disable read_4xio; 

7684       0/1     ==>  						SIR_Mode = 1'b0;

7685       0/1     ==>  						SG_Mode = 1'b0;

7686                    					  end
7687                    	        		else 
7688                    					  begin
7689       0/1     ==>            				SO_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7690       0/1     ==>            				SI_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7691       0/1     ==>            				WP_OUT_EN   = 1'b1;

7692       0/1     ==>            				SIO3_OUT_EN = 1'b1;

7693       0/1     ==>            				SO_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7694       0/1     ==>            				SI_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7695       0/1     ==>            				WP_IN_EN    = 1'b0;

7696       0/1     ==>            				SIO3_IN_EN  = 1'b0;

7697       0/1     ==>            				Read_Mode  	= 1'b1;

7698       0/1     ==>              			if ( Dummy_Count ) 

7699                    						  begin
7700       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;

7701       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV Dummy_Count ? OUT_Buf[7:4] : OUT_Buf[3:0];

7702                                			  end
7703                                			else 
7704                    						  begin
7705       0/1     ==>              				if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b00) && (Address[2:0]==3'b111) )

7706       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:3], 3'b000};

7707       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b01) && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7708       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7709       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode && (Address[3:0]==4'b1111) )

7710       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:4], 4'b0000};

7711       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b10) && (Address[4:0]==5'b1_1111) )

7712       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:5], 5'b0_0000};

7713       0/1     ==>                				else if ( RPARAM[2] && (RPARAM[1:0]==2'b11) && (Address[5:0]==6'b11_1111) )

7714       0/1     ==>                					Address = {Address[A_MSB:6], 6'b00_0000};

7715                                  				else
7716       0/1     ==>                					Address = Address + 1;

7717       0/1     ==>               				load_address(Address);

7718       0/1     ==>               				read_array(Address, OUT_Buf);

7719       0/1     ==>  							Dummy_Count = 1;

7720       0/1     ==>  							{SIO3_Reg, SIO2_Reg, SIO1_Reg, SIO0_Reg} <= #tV OUT_Buf[7:4];

7721                               				  end
7722                    	        		end
7723                    	    		end//forever  
7724                    			end
7725

7732                    //				  end
7733                    //			  end
7734                    		join
7735                    	  end
7736                    	  end
7737                        endtask // fastread_4xio

8081                    					  end
8082                    					end

8459                    					Dummy_Count = Dummy_Count - 1;
8460                    					SIO1_Reg <= #tV Prea_Reg_Out[Dummy_Count];
8461                    				end
8462                    				else begin
8463                    					Dummy_Count = 7;
8464                    					SIO1_Reg <= #tV Prea_Reg_Out[Dummy_Count];
8465                    				end
8466                    			end
8467                                    end
8468                                end
8469                            end
8470                         endtask
8471                    
8472                    */

8483                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
8484                        /*  Description: define read array output task                          */
8485                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
8486                        task read_array;
8487                            input [A_MSB:0] Address;
8488                            output [7:0]    OUT_Buf;
8489                            begin
8490       1/1                      if ( SIR_Mode == 1 ) begin
8491       0/2     ==>                  if 		(Address[13:12] == 2'b00) OUT_Buf = SIR0_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SIR:0]];

8492       0/2     ==>                  else if (Address[13:12] == 2'b01) OUT_Buf = SIR1_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SIR:0]];

8493       0/2     ==>                  else if (Address[13:12] == 2'b10) OUT_Buf = SIR2_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SIR:0]];

8494       0/2     ==>                  else if (Address[13:12] == 2'b11) OUT_Buf = SIR3_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SIR:0]];

                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
8495                                end
8496       1/1                      else if ( SG_Mode == 1 ) 
8497                      						begin
8498       0/1     ==>                  OUT_Buf = SG_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SG:0]];

8499                                	end
8500       1/1                      else if ( RDSFDP_Mode == 1 ) begin
8501       0/1     ==>                  OUT_Buf = SFDP_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_SFDP:0]];

8502                                end
8503       1/1                      else if ( RDUNIQ_Mode == 1 ) begin
8504       0/1     ==>                  OUT_Buf = UNIQ_ARRAY[Address[A_MSB_UNIQ:0]];

8505                                end
8506                                else begin
8507       1/1                          OUT_Buf = ARRAY[Address] ;
8508                                end
8509                            end
8510                        endtask //  read_array

8528                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/
8529                        /*	Description: define a safeguard_protect area function		    				*/
8530                        /*	INPUT: address							    																		*/
8531                        /*----------------------------------------------------------------------*/ 
8532                    		task safeguard;
8533                    				input [A_MSB:0] Address;
8534                    				output sg_protect;
8535                    				reg [7:0] SG_HBYTE;
8536                    				reg [7:0] SG_LBYTE;
8537                    				reg [A_MSB_SG:0] A_SG;
8538                    				begin
8539                    						//protect_define
8540       0/1     ==>  						if( (WRSR_Mode == 1'b1) || (WRFR_Mode == 1'b1) || (SG_Mode == 1'b1) || (SIR_Mode == 1'b1) ) 

8541                    							begin 
8542       0/1     ==>  									sg_protect = 1'b0; 

8543                    							end
8544       0/1     ==>  						else if ( (CE_Mode == 1'b1) || (BE64K_Mode == 1'b1) )

8545                    							begin
8546       0/1     ==>  									A_SG = {Address[A_MSB:16], 1'b0};

8547       0/1     ==>  									SG_LBYTE = SG_ARRAY[A_SG];

8548       0/1     ==>  									SG_HBYTE = SG_ARRAY[A_SG+1];

8549       0/2     ==>  									if ( &{SG_HBYTE,SG_LBYTE} == 1'b0 ) sg_protect = 1'b1;

8550       0/1     ==>  									else sg_protect = 1'b0;

8551                    							end
8552       0/1     ==>  						else if ( BE32K_Mode == 1'b1 )

8553                    							begin
8554       0/1     ==>  									A_SG = Address[A_MSB:15];

8555       0/1     ==>  									SG_LBYTE = SG_ARRAY[A_SG];

8556       0/2     ==>  									if ( &SG_LBYTE == 1'b0 ) sg_protect = 1'b1;

8557       0/1     ==>  									else sg_protect = 1'b0;

8558                    							end
8559       0/1     ==>  						else if ( (SER_Mode == 1'b1) || (PP_1XIO_Mode == 1'b1) || (PP_4XIO_Mode == 1'b1) )

8560                    							begin
8561       0/1     ==>  									A_SG = Address[A_MSB:15];

8562       0/1     ==>  									SG_LBYTE = SG_ARRAY[A_SG];

8563       0/2     ==>  									if ( SG_LBYTE[Address[14:12]] == 1'b0 ) sg_protect = 1'b1;

8564       0/1     ==>  									else sg_protect = 1'b0;

8565                    							end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
8566                    				end
8567                    		endtask // safeguard

9240                        integer AC_Check_File;
9241                        // timing check module 
9242                        initial 
9243                        begin 
9244                        	AC_Check_File= $fopen ("ac_check.err" );    
9245                        end
9246                    
9247                        time  T_CS_P , T_CS_N;
9248                        time  T_WP_P , T_WP_N;
9249                        time  T_SCLK_P , T_SCLK_N;
9250                        time  T_SIO3_P , T_SIO3_N;
9251                        time  T_SI;
9252                        time  T_SO;
9253                        time  T_WP;
9254                        time  T_SIO3;
9255                        time  T_RESET_N, T_RESET_P;                    
9256                    
9257                        initial 
9258                        begin
9259                    	T_CS_P = 0; 
9260                    	T_CS_N = 0;
9261                    	T_WP_P = 0;  
9262                    	T_WP_N = 0;
9263                    	T_SCLK_P = 0;  
9264                    	T_SCLK_N = 0;
9265                    	T_SIO3_P = 0;  
9266                    	T_SIO3_N = 0;
9267                    	T_SI = 0;
9268                    	T_SO = 0;
9269                    	T_WP = 0;
9270                    	T_SIO3 = 0;
9271                    	T_RESET_N = 0;
9272                    	T_RESET_P = 0;
9273                        end
9274                     
9275                    // Check SCLK period: must be longer than tSCLK; 
9276                        always @ ( posedge SCLK ) begin
9277                    	//tSCLK for fast frequency
9278       1/1                  if ( (($time - T_SCLK_P) < tSCLK) && ($time > 0) && ~CS ) 
9279       0/1     ==>  	    $fwrite (AC_Check_File, "Clock Frequence for except Normal READ struction fCT =%d Mhz, fCT timing violation at %d \n", fCT, $time );

                        MISSING_ELSE
9280                    	//tSCLK_NOR for normal read frequency
9281       1/1                  if ( (($time - T_SCLK_P) < tSCLK_NOR) && Read_1XIO_Chk && ($time > 0) && ~CS )
9282       0/1     ==>  	    $fwrite (AC_Check_File, "Clock Frequence for Normal READ instruction fC =%d Mhz, fC timing violation at %d \n", fC, $time );

                        MISSING_ELSE
9283                    
9284                    	//fTSCLK
9285       1/1          		if ( $time - T_SCLK_P < tTSCLK && Read_2XIO_Chk && RP_0000 && $time > 0 && ~CS )
9286       0/1     ==>  		$write (AC_Check_File, "Clock Frequence for 2XIO instruction fTSCLK =%d Mhz, fTSCLK timing violation at %d \n", fTSCLK, $time );

                        MISSING_ELSE
